. foreach fe of numlist $fe_list {
  2.     if `fe' == 1 {
  3.         reg countk ${downup_var} `controls', vce(cluster grid_id)
  4.         local monthyearfe "N"
  5.         local acfe "N"
  6.         local acmonthfe "N"
  7.         local gridfe "N"
  8.     }
  9.     else if `fe' == 2 {
 10.         reghdfejl countk ${downup_var} `controls', ///
>             absorb(monthyear#cohort) cluster(`cluster')
 11.         local monthyearfe "Y"
 12.         local acfe "N"
 13.         local acmonthfe "N"
 14.         local gridfe "N"
 15.     }
 16.     else if `fe' == 3 {
 17.         reghdfejl countk ${downup_var} `controls', ///
>             absorb(grid_id#cohort monthyear#cohort) cluster(`cluster')
 18.         local monthyearfe "Y"
 19.         local acfe "N"
 20.         local acmonthfe "N"
 21.         local gridfe "Y"
 22.     }
 23.     else if `fe' == 4 {
 24.         reghdfejl countk ${downup_var} `controls', ///
>             absorb(grid_id#cohort ac_id#monthyear#cohort) cluster(`cluster')
 25.         local monthyearfe "N"
 26.         local acfe "N"
 27.         local acmonthfe "Y"
 28.         local gridfe "Y"
 29.     }

 31.         display as error "Unsupported FE specification `fe'; use 1/4."
 32.         exit 198
 33.     }
 34. 
.     if e(N) != `common_n' {
 35.         display as error "FE specification `fe' changed the anchored estim
> ation sample."
 36.         exit 459
 37.     }

.     estadd scalar ymean = `ymean'
 39.     estadd scalar ymean2 = `ymean2'
 40.     estadd scalar acq = `numacs'

 42.     estadd scalar window_max = `window_max'
 43.     estadd local smpl "Rural"
 44.     estadd local fespec "`fespec`fe''"
 45.     estadd local monthyearfe "`monthyearfe'"
 46.     estadd local acfe "`acfe'"
 47.     estadd local acmonthfe "`acmonthfe'"
 48.     estadd local gridfe "`gridfe'"
 49.     local estimates `estimates' eq`fe'
 50.     est store eq`fe'
 51. }

. * drop if dpl_ac == 1
. * drop _merge
. 
. * Create count in thousands
. capture drop countk

. gen countk = count * 1000

. 
. * Filter data: year < 2022 or (year == 2022 & month <= 8)
. keep if year < 2022 | (year == 2022 & month <= 8)
(41,705 observations deleted)

. 
. * Sort data
. sort unique_small_grid_id monthyear

. 
. *****************************************************************************
> ***
. * Encode IDs
. *****************************************************************************
> ***
. 
. capture confirm numeric variable unique_small_grid_id

. if _rc {
.     encode unique_small_grid_id, gen(grid_id)
. }

. else {
.     gen grid_id = unique_small_grid_id
. }

. 
. capture confirm numeric variable ac_uq_id

. if _rc {
.     encode ac_uq_id, gen(ac_id)
. }

. else {
.     gen ac_id = ac_uq_id
. }

. 
. * Create cluster variable (stacked: interact with cohort)
. global cluster unique_small_grid_id#cohort ac_uq_id#cohort#monthyear

. 
. 
. * Count unique ACs
. egen tag_ac = tag(ac_id)

. count if tag_ac == 1
  796

. local numacs = r(N)

. 
. *****************************************************************************
> ***
. * Run Regressions by Agricultural Year (Sep-Aug)
. *****************************************************************************
> ***
. 
. global controls av_wind_speed wind_direction

. 
. * Store year labels for table header
. local yearlabels ""

. 
. local i = 1

. forvalues yr = 2012/2021 {
  2. 
.     local yr_next = `yr' + 1
  3. 
.     * Define agricultural year: Sep of yr to Aug of yr+1
.     * (year == yr & month >= 9) | (year == yr+1 & month <= 8)
. 
.         preserve
  4.                 keep if (year == `yr' & month >= 9) | (year == `yr_next' &
>  month <= 8)
  5. 
.                 * Project-standard treated-group pre-treatment means for this
>  subsample.
.                 gen relative_year_bin = floor(relative_monthyear / 12)
  6.                 gen moderator = 0
  7.                 quietly summarize countk if treat == 1 & relative_year_bin
>  <= -1
  8.                 local meandv`i' = r(mean)
  9.                 quietly summarize countk if treat == 1 & relative_year_bin
>  <= -1 & moderator == 1
 10.                 local meandv2`i' = r(mean)
 11. 
.                 * Run regression
.                 reghdfejl countk downup_ac_pop $controls , ///
>                         absorb(grid_id#cohort ac_id#monthyear#cohort) cluster
> ($cluster )
 12. 
.                 * Store statistics
.                 estadd local gridfe "Y"
 13.                 estadd local acmonthfe "Y"
 14.                 estadd scalar ymean = `meandv`i''
 15.                 estadd scalar ymean2 = `meandv2`i''
 16.                 estadd scalar acq = `numacs'
 17.                 est store eq`i'
 18.                 
.     restore
 19. 
.     * Build year label
.     local yearlabels "`yearlabels' `yr'/`yr_next'"
 20. 
.     local i = `i' + 1
 21. }

. capture program drop _fmt_num

. program define _fmt_num, rclass
  1.     args x
  2.     if missing(`x') {
  3.         return local out ""
  4.         exit
  5.     }
  6.     local out : display %15.3fc `x'
  7.     local out = strtrim("`out'")
  8.     while substr("`out'", -1, 1) == "0" & strpos("`out'", ".") > 0 {
  9.         local out = substr("`out'", 1, strlen("`out'") - 1)
 10.     }
 11.     if substr("`out'", -1, 1) == "." {
 12.         local out = substr("`out'", 1, strlen("`out'") - 1)
 13.     }
 14.     return local out "`out'"
 15. end

. 
. capture program drop _fmt_int

. program define _fmt_int, rclass
  1.     args x
  2.     if missing(`x') {
  3.         return local out ""
  4.         exit
  5.     }
  6.     local out : display %20.0fc `x'
  7.     local out = strtrim("`out'")
  8.     return local out "`out'"
  9. end

. 
. capture program drop _unique_count

. program define _unique_count, rclass
  1.     syntax varname
  2.     preserve
  3.         keep `varlist'
  4.         drop if missing(`varlist')
  5.         duplicates drop
  6.         count
  7.         return scalar n = r(N)
  8.     restore
  9. end

>     downup_ac_pop av_wind_speed wind_direction rice_prod_aclvl_ahigh

  9.         quietly _unique_count `v'
 10.         _fmt_int `r(n)'
 11.         local ufmt "`r(out)'"
 12.     }
 13.     else {
 14.         quietly summarize `v'
 15.         _fmt_num `r(mean)'
 16.         local meanfmt "`r(out)'"
 17.         _fmt_num `r(sd)'
 18.         local sdfmt "`r(out)'"
 19.         _fmt_num `r(min)'
 20.         local minfmt "`r(out)'"
 21.         _fmt_num `r(max)'
 22.         local maxfmt "`r(out)'"
 23.         quietly count if !missing(`v')
 24.         _fmt_int `r(N)'
 25.         local Nfmt "`r(out)'"
 26.     }
 27.     local vlabel "`lab_`v''"
 28.     file write texout "`vlabel' & `meanfmt' & `sdfmt' & `minfmt' & `maxfmt
> ' & `Nfmt' & `ufmt'\\\\" _n
 29. }
